+---
+name: the-way-critique
+description: Use when an agent's proposal needs an independent critical reading, when a complete set of proposals will be reviewed together by a human, or when a change to an invariant test or to the foundation needs its independent assessment. CREATE AGENTS.md

+++ AGENTS.md
@@ -0,0 +1,15 @@
+<!-- the-way:begin -->
+# Pinned the-way process
+
+This repository uses the-way, the process for its foundation, at the commit the gitlink of the Git submodule `.the-way` records. After a fresh clone run `git submodule update --init -- .the-way`; it needs read access to the Git URL in `.gitmodules`. Before use, `git -C .the-way rev-parse HEAD` must show the commit `git ls-files --stage -- .the-way` records. If initialization fails or the commits differ, stop; never use another copy or version of the-way.
+
+Write foundation entries only through the project skills `the-way-record-decision`, `the-way-record-invariant`, `the-way-record-glossary-term`, `the-way-update-part-map`, `the-way-record-environment-fact`. Use the project skills `the-way-find-foundation-entries`, `the-way-define-project`, `the-way-plan-project`, `the-way-lead-project`, `the-way-settle-technology`, `the-way-refine-task`, `the-way-implement-task`, `the-way-plan-task`, `the-way-critique`, `the-way-maintain-styleguide` for their named work. Where a skill or a file it reads names one without the `the-way-` prefix, use the prefixed project skill, never a personal skill of that name.
+
+Run the-way's scripts from this repository's root as `.the-way/<path>` and always name this repository's file or directory, e.g. `python -B .the-way/gate/run_tests.py <directory>` and `python -B .the-way/elements/<element>/format_guard.py foundations/<file>`. A guard run inside `.the-way` without a file checks the-way's own files. Records of a run (`--record`) go under `.runs/`, which `.gitignore` must keep ignored.
+
+The main branch is the branch task branches merge into. In a clone, `git symbolic-ref --short refs/remotes/origin/HEAD` names it as `origin/<branch>`; otherwise ask the human. On a task branch pass `--base` the commit `git merge-base HEAD <main branch>` prints and `--merge-onto` the commit of the main branch the merge goes onto (the part map's guard takes no `--merge-onto`). Pass `none` only while this repository has no main branch at all; a command that cannot find the branch means a wrong name, not a missing branch. With `none` the checks that need the commit stay unchecked.
+
+Until the part map has its first parts, its guard reports every tracked file as claimed by no part; that is expected, and it is not a pass.
+
+Nothing runs these checks automatically. Before a merge, run the format guard of every changed foundation file and the tests of every changed part, and report what failed and what stayed unchecked. Where this repository holds `guidelines/`, run `python -B .the-way/product-guidelines/form_check.py` and `python -B .the-way/product-guidelines/form_check.py --merge-onto <main commit>` before every merge. A check that did not run is never a pass.
+<!-- the-way:end -->

CREATE AGENTS.md
--- /dev/null
+++ AGENTS.md
@@ -0,0 +1,15 @@
+<!-- the-way:begin -->
+# Pinned the-way process
+
+This repository uses the-way, the process for its foundation, at the commit the gitlink of the Git submodule `.the-way` records. After a fresh clone run `git submodule update --init -- .the-way`; it needs read access to the Git URL in `.gitmodules`. Before use, `git -C .the-way rev-parse HEAD` must show the commit `git ls-files --stage -- .the-way` records. If initialization fails or the commits differ, stop; never use another copy or version of the-way.
+
+Write foundation entries only through the project skills `the-way-record-decision`, `the-way-record-invariant`, `the-way-record-glossary-term`, `the-way-update-part-map`, `the-way-record-environment-fact`. Use the project skills `the-way-find-foundation-entries`, `the-way-define-project`, `the-way-plan-project`, `the-way-lead-project`, `the-way-settle-technology`, `the-way-refine-task`, `the-way-implement-task`, `the-way-plan-task`, `the-way-critique`, `the-way-maintain-styleguide` for their named work. Where a skill or a file it reads names one without the `the-way-` prefix, use the prefixed project skill, never a personal skill of that name.
+
+Run the-way's scripts from this repository's root as `.the-way/<path>` and always name this repository's file or directory, e.g. `python -B .the-way/gate/run_tests.py <directory>` and `python -B .the-way/elements/<element>/format_guard.py foundations/<file>`. A guard run inside `.the-way` without a file checks the-way's own files. Records of a run (`--record`) go under `.runs/`, which `.gitignore` must keep ignored.
+
+The main branch is the branch task branches merge into. In a clone, `git symbolic-ref --short refs/remotes/origin/HEAD` names it as `origin/<branch>`; otherwise ask the human. On a task branch pass `--base` the commit `git merge-base HEAD <main branch>` prints and `--merge-onto` the commit of the main branch the merge goes onto (the part map's guard takes no `--merge-onto`). Pass `none` only while this repository has no main branch at all; a command that cannot find the branch means a wrong name, not a missing branch. With `none` the checks that need the commit stay unchecked.
+
+Until the part map has its first parts, its guard reports every tracked file as claimed by no part; that is expected, and it is not a pass.
+
+Nothing runs these checks automatically. Before a merge, run the format guard of every changed foundation file and the tests of every changed part, and report what failed and what stayed unchecked. Where this repository holds `guidelines/`, run `python -B .the-way/product-guidelines/form_check.py` and `python -B .the-way/product-guidelines/form_check.py --merge-onto <main commit>` before every merge. A check that did not run is never a pass.
+<!-- the-way:end -->

@@ -0,0 +1,15 @@
+<!-- the-way:begin -->
+# Pinned the-way process
+
+This repository uses the-way, the process for its foundation, at the commit the gitlink of the Git submodule `.the-way` records. After a fresh clone run `git submodule update --init -- .the-way`; it needs read access to the Git URL in `.gitmodules`. Before use, `git -C .the-way rev-parse HEAD` must show the commit `git ls-files --stage -- .the-way` records. If initialization fails or the commits differ, stop; never use another copy or version of the-way.
+
+Write foundation entries only through the project skills `the-way-record-decision`, `the-way-record-invariant`, `the-way-record-glossary-term`, `the-way-update-part-map`, `the-way-record-environment-fact`. Use the project skills `the-way-find-foundation-entries`, `the-way-define-project`, `the-way-plan-project`, `the-way-lead-project`, `the-way-settle-technology`, `the-way-refine-task`, `the-way-implement-task`, `the-way-plan-task`, `the-way-critique`, `the-way-maintain-styleguide` for their named work. Where a skill or a file it reads names one without the `the-way-` prefix, use the prefixed project skill, never a personal skill of that name.
+
+Run the-way's scripts from this repository's root as `.the-way/<path>` and always name this repository's file or directory, e.g. `python -B .the-way/gate/run_tests.py <directory>` and `python -B .the-way/elements/<element>/format_guard.py foundations/<file>`. A guard run inside `.the-way` without a file checks the-way's own files. Records of a run (`--record`) go under `.runs/`, which `.gitignore` must keep ignored.
+
+The main branch is the branch task branches merge into. In a clone, `git symbolic-ref --short refs/remotes/origin/HEAD` names it as `origin/<branch>`; otherwise ask the human. On a task branch pass `--base` the commit `git merge-base HEAD <main branch>` prints and `--merge-onto` the commit of the main branch the merge goes onto (the part map's guard takes no `--merge-onto`). Pass `none` only while this repository has no main branch at all; a command that cannot find the branch means a wrong name, not a missing branch. With `none` the checks that need the commit stay unchecked.
+
+Until the part map has its first parts, its guard reports every tracked file as claimed by no part; that is expected, and it is not a pass.
+
+Nothing runs these checks automatically. Before a merge, run the format guard of every changed foundation file and the tests of every changed part, and report what failed and what stayed unchecked. Where this repository holds `guidelines/`, run `python -B .the-way/product-guidelines/form_check.py` and `python -B .the-way/product-guidelines/form_check.py --merge-onto <main commit>` before every merge. A check that did not run is never a pass.
+<!-- the-way:end -->

CREATE AGENTS.md

@@ -0,0 +1,15 @@
+<!-- the-way:begin -->
+# Pinned the-way process
+
+This repository uses the-way, the process for its foundation, at the commit the gitlink of the Git submodule `.the-way` records. After a fresh clone run `git submodule update --init -- .the-way`; it needs read access to the Git URL in `.gitmodules`. Before use, `git -C .the-way rev-parse HEAD` must show the commit `git ls-files --stage -- .the-way` records. If initialization fails or the commits differ, stop; never use another copy or version of the-way.
+
+Write foundation entries only through the project skills `the-way-record-decision`, `the-way-record-invariant`, `the-way-record-glossary-term`, `the-way-update-part-map`, `the-way-record-environment-fact`. Use the project skills `the-way-find-foundation-entries`, `the-way-define-project`, `the-way-plan-project`, `the-way-lead-project`, `the-way-settle-technology`, `the-way-refine-task`, `the-way-implement-task`, `the-way-plan-task`, `the-way-critique`, `the-way-maintain-styleguide` for their named work. Where a skill or a file it reads names one without the `the-way-` prefix, use the prefixed project skill, never a personal skill of that name.
+
+Run the-way's scripts from this repository's root as `.the-way/<path>` and always name this repository's file or directory, e.g. `python -B .the-way/gate/run_tests.py <directory>` and `python -B .the-way/elements/<element>/format_guard.py foundations/<file>`. A guard run inside `.the-way` without a file checks the-way's own files. Records of a run (`--record`) go under `.runs/`, which `.gitignore` must keep ignored.
+
+The main branch is the branch task branches merge into. In a clone, `git symbolic-ref --short refs/remotes/origin/HEAD` names it as `origin/<branch>`; otherwise ask the human. On a task branch pass `--base` the commit `git merge-base HEAD <main branch>` prints and `--merge-onto` the commit of the main branch the merge goes onto (the part map's guard takes no `--merge-onto`). Pass `none` only while this repository has no main branch at all; a command that cannot find the branch means a wrong name, not a missing branch. With `none` the checks that need the commit stay unchecked.
+
+Until the part map has its first parts, its guard reports every tracked file as claimed by no part; that is expected, and it is not a pass.
+
+Nothing runs these checks automatically. Before a merge, run the format guard of every changed foundation file and the tests of every changed part, and report what failed and what stayed unchecked. Where this repository holds `guidelines/`, run `python -B .the-way/product-guidelines/form_check.py` and `python -B .the-way/product-guidelines/form_check.py --merge-onto <main commit>` before every merge. A check that did not run is never a pass.
+<!-- the-way:end -->

@@ -0,0 +1,15 @@
+<!-- the-way:begin -->
+# Pinned the-way process
+
+This repository uses the-way, the process for its foundation, at the commit the gitlink of the Git submodule `.the-way` records. After a fresh clone run `git submodule update --init -- .the-way`; it needs read access to the Git URL in `.gitmodules`. Before use, `git -C .the-way rev-parse HEAD` must show the commit `git ls-files --stage -- .the-way` records. If initialization fails or the commits differ, stop; never use another copy or version of the-way.
+
+Write foundation entries only through the project skills `the-way-record-decision`, `the-way-record-invariant`, `the-way-record-glossary-term`, `the-way-update-part-map`, `the-way-record-environment-fact`. Use the project skills `the-way-find-foundation-entries`, `the-way-define-project`, `the-way-plan-project`, `the-way-lead-project`, `the-way-settle-technology`, `the-way-refine-task`, `the-way-implement-task`, `the-way-plan-task`, `the-way-critique`, `the-way-maintain-styleguide` for their named work. Where a skill or a file it reads names one without the `the-way-` prefix, use the prefixed project skill, never a personal skill of that name.
+
+Run the-way's scripts from this repository's root as `.the-way/<path>` and always name this repository's file or directory, e.g. `python -B .the-way/gate/run_tests.py <directory>` and `python -B .the-way/elements/<element>/format_guard.py foundations/<file>`. A guard run inside `.the-way` without a file checks the-way's own files. Records of a run (`--record`) go under `.runs/`, which `.gitignore` must keep ignored.
+
+The main branch is the branch task branches merge into. In a clone, `git symbolic-ref --short refs/remotes/origin/HEAD` names it as `origin/<branch>`; otherwise ask the human. On a task branch pass `--base` the commit `git merge-base HEAD <main branch>` prints and `--merge-onto` the commit of the main branch the merge goes onto (the part map's guard takes no `--merge-onto`). Pass `none` only while this repository has no main branch at all; a command that cannot find the branch means a wrong name, not a missing branch. With `none` the checks that need the commit stay unchecked.
+
+Until the part map has its first parts, its guard reports every tracked file as claimed by no part; that is expected, and it is not a pass.
+
+Nothing runs these checks automatically. Before a merge, run the format guard of every changed foundation file and the tests of every changed part, and report what failed and what stayed unchecked. Where this repository holds `guidelines/`, run `python -B .the-way/product-guidelines/form_check.py` and `python -B .the-way/product-guidelines/form_check.py --merge-onto <main commit>` before every merge. A check that did not run is never a pass.
+<!-- the-way:end -->

+# Invariants
+
+## Withdrawn identifiers
+
+| Identifier | Why | Successors |
+|---|---|---|
+
+## Quarantined invariants
+
+| Identifier | Version | Until |
+|---|---|---|
+
+## Recovered invariants
+
+| Identifier | Version | Searched | Found |
+|---|---|---|---|

+<!-- the-way:begin -->
+# Pinned the-way process
+
+This repository uses the-way, the process for its foundation, at the commit the gitlink of the Git submodule `.the-way` records. After a fresh clone run `git submodule update --init -- .the-way`; it needs read access to the Git URL in `.gitmodules`. Before use, `git -C .the-way rev-parse HEAD` must show the commit `git ls-files --stage -- .the-way` records. If initialization fails or the commits differ, stop; never use another copy or version of the-way.
+
+Write foundation entries only through the project skills `the-way-record-decision`, `the-way-record-invariant`, `the-way-record-glossary-term`, `the-way-update-part-map`, `the-way-record-environment-fact`. Use the project skills `the-way-find-foundation-entries`, `the-way-define-project`, `the-way-plan-project`, `the-way-lead-project`, `the-way-settle-technology`, `the-way-refine-task`, `the-way-implement-task`, `the-way-plan-task`, `the-way-critique`, `the-way-maintain-styleguide` for their named work. Where a skill or a file it reads names one without the `the-way-` prefix, use the prefixed project skill, never a personal skill of that name.
+
+Run the-way's scripts from this repository's root as `.the-way/<path>` and always name this repository's file or directory, e.g. `python -B .the-way/gate/run_tests.py <directory>` and `python -B .the-way/elements/<element>/format_guard.py foundations/<file>`. A guard run inside `.the-way` without a file checks the-way's own files. Records of a run (`--record`) go under `.runs/`, which `.gitignore` must keep ignored.
+
+The main branch is the branch task branches merge into. In a clone, `git symbolic-ref --short refs/remotes/origin/HEAD` names it as `origin/<branch>`; otherwise ask the human. On a task branch pass `--base` the commit `git merge-base HEAD <main branch>` prints and `--merge-onto` the commit of the main branch the merge goes onto (the part map's guard takes no `--merge-onto`). Pass `none` only while this repository has no main branch at all; a command that cannot find the branch means a wrong name, not a missing branch. With `none` the checks that need the commit stay unchecked.
+
+Until the part map has its first parts, its guard reports every tracked file as claimed by no part; that is expected, and it is not a pass.
+
+Nothing runs these checks automatically. Before a merge, run the format guard of every changed foundation file and the tests of every changed part, and report what failed and what stayed unchecked. Where this repository holds `guidelines/`, run `python -B .the-way/product-guidelines/form_check.py` and `python -B .the-way/product-guidelines/form_check.py --merge-onto <main commit>` before every merge. A check that did not run is never a pass.
+<!-- the-way:end -->

+++ AGENTS.md
@@ -0,0 +1,15 @@
+<!-- the-way:begin -->
+# Pinned the-way process
+
+This repository uses the-way, the process for its foundation, at the commit the gitlink of the Git submodule `.the-way` records. After a fresh clone run `git submodule update --init -- .the-way`; it needs read access to the Git URL in `.gitmodules`. Before use, `git -C .the-way rev-parse HEAD` must show the commit `git ls-files --stage -- .the-way` records. If initialization fails or the commits differ, stop; never use another copy or version of the-way.
+
+Write foundation entries only through the project skills `the-way-record-decision`, `the-way-record-invariant`, `the-way-record-glossary-term`, `the-way-update-part-map`, `the-way-record-environment-fact`. Use the project skills `the-way-find-foundation-entries`, `the-way-define-project`, `the-way-plan-project`, `the-way-lead-project`, `the-way-settle-technology`, `the-way-refine-task`, `the-way-implement-task`, `the-way-plan-task`, `the-way-critique`, `the-way-maintain-styleguide` for their named work. Where a skill or a file it reads names one without the `the-way-` prefix, use the prefixed project skill, never a personal skill of that name.
+
+Run the-way's scripts from this repository's root as `.the-way/<path>` and always name this repository's file or directory, e.g. `python -B .the-way/gate/run_tests.py <directory>` and `python -B .the-way/elements/<element>/format_guard.py foundations/<file>`. A guard run inside `.the-way` without a file checks the-way's own files. Records of a run (`--record`) go under `.runs/`, which `.gitignore` must keep ignored.
+
+The main branch is the branch task branches merge into. In a clone, `git symbolic-ref --short refs/remotes/origin/HEAD` names it as `origin/<branch>`; otherwise ask the human. On a task branch pass `--base` the commit `git merge-base HEAD <main branch>` prints and `--merge-onto` the commit of the main branch the merge goes onto (the part map's guard takes no `--merge-onto`). Pass `none` only while this repository has no main branch at all; a command that cannot find the branch means a wrong name, not a missing branch. With `none` the checks that need the commit stay unchecked.
+
+Until the part map has its first parts, its guard reports every tracked file as claimed by no part; that is expected, and it is not a pass.
+
+Nothing runs these checks automatically. Before a merge, run the format guard of every changed foundation file and the tests of every changed part, and report what failed and what stayed unchecked. Where this repository holds `guidelines/`, run `python -B .the-way/product-guidelines/form_check.py` and `python -B .the-way/product-guidelines/form_check.py --merge-onto <main commit>` before every merge. A check that did not run is never a pass.
+<!-- the-way:end -->

CREATE AGENTS.md

+++ AGENTS.md
@@ -0,0 +1,15 @@
+<!-- the-way:begin -->
+# Pinned the-way process
+
+This repository uses the-way, the process for its foundation, at the commit the gitlink of the Git submodule `.the-way` records. After a fresh clone run `git submodule update --init -- .the-way`; it needs read access to the Git URL in `.gitmodules`. Before use, `git -C .the-way rev-parse HEAD` must show the commit `git ls-files --stage -- .the-way` records. If initialization fails or the commits differ, stop; never use another copy or version of the-way.
+
+Write foundation entries only through the project skills `the-way-record-decision`, `the-way-record-invariant`, `the-way-record-glossary-term`, `the-way-update-part-map`, `the-way-record-environment-fact`. Use the project skills `the-way-find-foundation-entries`, `the-way-define-project`, `the-way-plan-project`, `the-way-lead-project`, `the-way-settle-technology`, `the-way-refine-task`, `the-way-implement-task`, `the-way-plan-task`, `the-way-critique`, `the-way-maintain-styleguide` for their named work. Where a skill or a file it reads names one without the `the-way-` prefix, use the prefixed project skill, never a personal skill of that name.
+
+Run the-way's scripts from this repository's root as `.the-way/<path>` and always name this repository's file or directory, e.g. `python -B .the-way/gate/run_tests.py <directory>` and `python -B .the-way/elements/<element>/format_guard.py foundations/<file>`. A guard run inside `.the-way` without a file checks the-way's own files. Records of a run (`--record`) go under `.runs/`, which `.gitignore` must keep ignored.
+
+The main branch is the branch task branches merge into. In a clone, `git symbolic-ref --short refs/remotes/origin/HEAD` names it as `origin/<branch>`; otherwise ask the human. On a task branch pass `--base` the commit `git merge-base HEAD <main branch>` prints and `--merge-onto` the commit of the main branch the merge goes onto (the part map's guard takes no `--merge-onto`). Pass `none` only while this repository has no main branch at all; a command that cannot find the branch means a wrong name, not a missing branch. With `none` the checks that need the commit stay unchecked.
+
+Until the part map has its first parts, its guard reports every tracked file as claimed by no part; that is expected, and it is not a pass.
+
+Nothing runs these checks automatically. Before a merge, run the format guard of every changed foundation file and the tests of every changed part, and report what failed and what stayed unchecked. Where this repository holds `guidelines/`, run `python -B .the-way/product-guidelines/form_check.py` and `python -B .the-way/product-guidelines/form_check.py --merge-onto <main commit>` before every merge. A check that did not run is never a pass.
+<!-- the-way:end -->

CREATE AGENTS.md

+++ AGENTS.md
@@ -0,0 +1,15 @@
+<!-- the-way:begin -->
+# Pinned the-way process
+
+This repository uses the-way, the process for its foundation, at the commit the gitlink of the Git submodule `.the-way` records. After a fresh clone run `git submodule update --init -- .the-way`; it needs read access to the Git URL in `.gitmodules`. Before use, `git -C .the-way rev-parse HEAD` must show the commit `git ls-files --stage -- .the-way` records. If initialization fails or the commits differ, stop; never use another copy or version of the-way.
+
+Write foundation entries only through the project skills `the-way-record-decision`, `the-way-record-invariant`, `the-way-record-glossary-term`, `the-way-update-part-map`, `the-way-record-environment-fact`. Use the project skills `the-way-find-foundation-entries`, `the-way-define-project`, `the-way-plan-project`, `the-way-lead-project`, `the-way-settle-technology`, `the-way-refine-task`, `the-way-implement-task`, `the-way-plan-task`, `the-way-critique`, `the-way-maintain-styleguide` for their named work. Where a skill or a file it reads names one without the `the-way-` prefix, use the prefixed project skill, never a personal skill of that name.
+
+Run the-way's scripts from this repository's root as `.the-way/<path>` and always name this repository's file or directory, e.g. `python -B .the-way/gate/run_tests.py <directory>` and `python -B .the-way/elements/<element>/format_guard.py foundations/<file>`. A guard run inside `.the-way` without a file checks the-way's own files. Records of a run (`--record`) go under `.runs/`, which `.gitignore` must keep ignored.
+
+The main branch is the branch task branches merge into. In a clone, `git symbolic-ref --short refs/remotes/origin/HEAD` names it as `origin/<branch>`; otherwise ask the human. On a task branch pass `--base` the commit `git merge-base HEAD <main branch>` prints and `--merge-onto` the commit of the main branch the merge goes onto (the part map's guard takes no `--merge-onto`). Pass `none` only while this repository has no main branch at all; a command that cannot find the branch means a wrong name, not a missing branch. With `none` the checks that need the commit stay unchecked.
+
+Until the part map has its first parts, its guard reports every tracked file as claimed by no part; that is expected, and it is not a pass.
+
+Nothing runs these checks automatically. Before a merge, run the format guard of every changed foundation file and the tests of every changed part, and report what failed and what stayed unchecked. Where this repository holds `guidelines/`, run `python -B .the-way/product-guidelines/form_check.py` and `python -B .the-way/product-guidelines/form_check.py --merge-onto <main commit>` before every merge. A check that did not run is never a pass.
+<!-- the-way:end -->

+<!-- the-way:begin -->
+# Pinned the-way process
+
+This repository uses the-way, the process for its foundation, at the commit the gitlink of the Git submodule `.the-way` records. After a fresh clone run `git submodule update --init -- .the-way`; it needs read access to the Git URL in `.gitmodules`. Before use, `git -C .the-way rev-parse HEAD` must show the commit `git ls-files --stage -- .the-way` records. If initialization fails or the commits differ, stop; never use another copy or version of the-way.
+
+Write foundation entries only through the project skills `the-way-record-decision`, `the-way-record-invariant`, `the-way-record-glossary-term`, `the-way-update-part-map`, `the-way-record-environment-fact`. Use the project skills `the-way-find-foundation-entries`, `the-way-define-project`, `the-way-plan-project`, `the-way-lead-project`, `the-way-settle-technology`, `the-way-refine-task`, `the-way-implement-task`, `the-way-plan-task`, `the-way-critique`, `the-way-maintain-styleguide` for their named work. Where a skill or a file it reads names one without the `the-way-` prefix, use the prefixed project skill, never a personal skill of that name.
+
+Run the-way's scripts from this repository's root as `.the-way/<path>` and always name this repository's file or directory, e.g. `python -B .the-way/gate/run_tests.py <directory>` and `python -B .the-way/elements/<element>/format_guard.py foundations/<file>`. A guard run inside `.the-way` without a file checks the-way's own files. Records of a run (`--record`) go under `.runs/`, which `.gitignore` must keep ignored.
+
+The main branch is the branch task branches merge into. In a clone, `git symbolic-ref --short refs/remotes/origin/HEAD` names it as `origin/<branch>`; otherwise ask the human. On a task branch pass `--base` the commit `git merge-base HEAD <main branch>` prints and `--merge-onto` the commit of the main branch the merge goes onto (the part map's guard takes no `--merge-onto`). Pass `none` only while this repository has no main branch at all; a command that cannot find the branch means a wrong name, not a missing branch. With `none` the checks that need the commit stay unchecked.
+
+Until the part map has its first parts, its guard reports every tracked file as claimed by no part; that is expected, and it is not a pass.
+
+Nothing runs these checks automatically. Before a merge, run the format guard of every changed foundation file and the tests of every changed part, and report what failed and what stayed unchecked. Where this repository holds `guidelines/`, run `python -B .the-way/product-guidelines/form_check.py` and `python -B .the-way/product-guidelines/form_check.py --merge-onto <main commit>` before every merge. A check that did not run is never a pass.
+<!-- the-way:end -->

+---
+name: the-way-record-decision
+description: Use when a decision has to be written into a product's decision log, foundations/decisions.md — a choice just settled in conversation that binds later work, a new answer to a question the log already holds, a decision recovered from existing code, a due review of a decision whose review-when date has come, or a decision a project definition projects. Use this product's pinned the-way commit.

+

+<!-- the-way:begin -->
+# Pinned the-way process
+
+This repository uses the-way, the process for its foundation, at the commit the gitlink of the Git submodule `.the-way` records. After a fresh clone run `git submodule update --init -- .the-way`; it needs read access to the Git URL in `.gitmodules`. Before use, `git -C .the-way rev-parse HEAD` must show the commit `git ls-files --stage -- .the-way` records. If initialization fails or the commits differ, stop; never use another copy or version of the-way.
+
+Write foundation entries only through the project skills `the-way-record-decision`, `the-way-record-invariant`, `the-way-record-glossary-term`, `the-way-update-part-map`, `the-way-record-environment-fact`. Use the project skills `the-way-find-foundation-entries`, `the-way-define-project`, `the-way-plan-project`, `the-way-lead-project`, `the-way-settle-technology`, `the-way-refine-task`, `the-way-implement-task`, `the-way-plan-task`, `the-way-critique`, `the-way-maintain-styleguide` for their named work. Where a skill or a file it reads names one without the `the-way-` prefix, use the prefixed project skill, never a personal skill of that name.
+
+Run the-way's scripts from this repository's root as `.the-way/<path>` and always name this repository's file or directory, e.g. `python -B .the-way/gate/run_tests.py <directory>` and `python -B .the-way/elements/<element>/format_guard.py foundations/<file>`. A guard run inside `.the-way` without a file checks the-way's own files. Records of a run (`--record`) go under `.runs/`, which `.gitignore` must keep ignored.
+
+The main branch is the branch task branches merge into. In a clone, `git symbolic-ref --short refs/remotes/origin/HEAD` names it as `origin/<branch>`; otherwise ask the human. On a task branch pass `--base` the commit `git merge-base HEAD <main branch>` prints and `--merge-onto` the commit of the main branch the merge goes onto (the part map's guard takes no `--merge-onto`). Pass `none` only while this repository has no main branch at all; a command that cannot find the branch means a wrong name, not a missing branch. With `none` the checks that need the commit stay unchecked.
+
+Until the part map has its first parts, its guard reports every tracked file as claimed by no part; that is expected, and it is not a pass.
+
+Nothing runs these checks automatically. Before a merge, run the format guard of every changed foundation file and the tests of every changed part, and report what failed and what stayed unchecked. Where this repository holds `guidelines/`, run `python -B .the-way/product-guidelines/form_check.py` and `python -B .the-way/product-guidelines/form_check.py --merge-onto <main commit>` before every merge. A check that did not run is never a pass.
+<!-- the-way:end -->

+<!-- the-way:begin -->
+# Pinned the-way process
+
+This repository uses the-way, the process for its foundation, at the commit the gitlink of the Git submodule `.the-way` records. After a fresh clone run `git submodule update --init -- .the-way`; it needs read access to the Git URL in `.gitmodules`. Before use, `git -C .the-way rev-parse HEAD` must show the commit `git ls-files --stage -- .the-way` records. If initialization fails or the commits differ, stop; never use another copy or version of the-way.
+
+Write foundation entries only through the project skills `the-way-record-decision`, `the-way-record-invariant`, `the-way-record-glossary-term`, `the-way-update-part-map`, `the-way-record-environment-fact`. Use the project skills `the-way-find-foundation-entries`, `the-way-define-project`, `the-way-plan-project`, `the-way-lead-project`, `the-way-settle-technology`, `the-way-refine-task`, `the-way-implement-task`, `the-way-plan-task`, `the-way-critique`, `the-way-maintain-styleguide` for their named work. Where a skill or a file it reads names one without the `the-way-` prefix, use the prefixed project skill, never a personal skill of that name.
+
+Run the-way's scripts from this repository's root as `.the-way/<path>` and always name this repository's file or directory, e.g. `python -B .the-way/gate/run_tests.py <directory>` and `python -B .the-way/elements/<element>/format_guard.py foundations/<file>`. A guard run inside `.the-way` without a file checks the-way's own files. Records of a run (`--record`) go under `.runs/`, which `.gitignore` must keep ignored.
+
+The main branch is the branch task branches merge into. In a clone, `git symbolic-ref --short refs/remotes/origin/HEAD` names it as `origin/<branch>`; otherwise ask the human. On a task branch pass `--base` the commit `git merge-base HEAD <main branch>` prints and `--merge-onto` the commit of the main branch the merge goes onto (the part map's guard takes no `--merge-onto`). Pass `none` only while this repository has no main branch at all; a command that cannot find the branch means a wrong name, not a missing branch. With `none` the checks that need the commit stay unchecked.
+
+Until the part map has its first parts, its guard reports every tracked file as claimed by no part; that is expected, and it is not a pass.
+
+Nothing runs these checks automatically. Before a merge, run the format guard of every changed foundation file and the tests of every changed part, and report what failed and what stayed unchecked. Where this repository holds `guidelines/`, run `python -B .the-way/product-guidelines/form_check.py` and `python -B .the-way/product-guidelines/form_check.py --merge-onto <main commit>` before every merge. A check that did not run is never a pass.
+<!-- the-way:end -->

After a fresh clone run `git submodule update --init -- .the-way`; it needs read access to the Git URL in `.gitmodules`. Before use, `git -C .the-way rev-parse HEAD` must show the commit `git ls-files --stage -- .the-way` records. If initialization fails or the commits differ, stop; never use another copy or version of the-way.\n+\n+Write foundation entries only through the project skills `the-way-record-decision`, `the-way-record-invariant`, `the-way-record-glossary-term`, `the-way-update-part-map`, `the-way-record-environment-fact`.
